{"parentUuid":"46ec0b71-811b-4e7a-a73f-b54ee32e0f81","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"ab3d21b156434cfd0","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018bHoDcnsH5yVVFMnGsDew1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Stripe Checkout Session Creation API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/checkout/create-session\n     5→ * Creates a Stripe Checkout session for payment processing\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';\n     9→import { stripe } from '@/lib/stripe';\n    10→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin';\n    11→import { STORE_DEFAULTS, ALLOWED_SHIPPING_COUNTRIES, BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config';\n    12→\n    13→/**\n    14→ * POST /api/checkout/create-session\n    15→ *\n    16→ * Create a Stripe Checkout Session for cart checkout\n    17→ *\n    18→ * Request body:\n    19→ * @param {Array} cartItems - Array of cart items with productId, variantId, quantity, personalization\n    20→ * @param {Object} shippingAddress - Shipping address (name, line1, city, state, postal_code, country)\n    21→ * @param {string} locale - User locale (en/es/de, default: en)\n    22→ * @param {string} currency - Currency code (default: EUR)\n    23→ * @param {string} customerEmail - Customer email address\n    24→ * @param {string} gift_message - Optional gift message\n    25→ *\n    26→ * @returns {Object} JSON response with Stripe Checkout Session URL\n    27→ */\n    28→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    29→  try {\n    30→    // Rate limit checkout session creation (5 per minute per IP)\n    31→    const { checkoutLimiter } = await import('@/lib/rate-limit')\n    32→    const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown'\n    33→    const { success } = checkoutLimiter.check(ip)\n    34→    if (!success) {\n    35→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Too many requests. Please wait a moment.' }, { status: 429 })\n    36→    }\n    37→\n    38→    const body = await req.json();\n    39→    const { cartItems, shippingAddress, locale = 'en', currency = STORE_DEFAULTS.stripeCurrency, customerEmail, gift_message, couponCode } = body;\n    40→\n    41→    // Validate required fields\n    42→    if (!cartItems || !Array.isArray(cartItems) || cartItems.length === 0) {\n    43→      return NextResponse.json(\n    44→        { error: 'Cart items are required' },\n    45→        { status: 400 }\n    46→      );\n    47→    }\n    48→\n    49→    // Validate all items have variant_id (required for Printify fulfillment)\n    50→    const itemsWithoutVariant = cartItems.filter(\n    51→      (item: any) => !item.variant_id\n    52→    );\n    53→    if (itemsWithoutVariant.length > 0) {\n    54→      return NextResponse.json(\n    55→        {\n    56→          error: 'Incomplete cart items',\n    57→          code: 'MISSING_VARIANTS',\n    58→          message: 'All items require a variant selection (size/color).',\n    59→          items: itemsWithoutVariant.map((i: any) => i.product_id),\n    60→        },\n    61→        { status: 400 }\n    62→      );\n    63→    }\n    64→\n    65→    // Validate all products exist and are active\n    66→    const productIds = [...new Set(cartItems.map((item: any) => item.product_id).filter(Boolean))];\n    67→\n    68→    // Verify all products are active\n    69→    if (productIds.length > 0) {\n    70→      const { data: activeProducts } = await supabaseAdmin\n    71→        .from('products')\n    72→        .select('id')\n    73→        .in('id', productIds)\n    74→        .eq('status', 'active')\n    75→        .is('deleted_at', null);\n    76→\n    77→     1→/**\n     2→ * Stripe Webhook Handler\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/webhooks/stripe\n     5→ * Handles Stripe webhook events (checkout.session.completed, etc.)\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     9→import { headers } from 'next/headers'\n    10→import { stripe } from '@/lib/stripe'\n    11→import { getProvider, initializeProviders } from '@/lib/pod'\n    12→import { canonicalAddressFromStripe } from '@/lib/pod/printify/mapper'\n    13→import { sendOrderConfirmationEmail, sendCreditPurchaseEmail } from '@/lib/resend'\n    14→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    15→import { triggerDripSequence } from '@/lib/email-drip'\n    16→import { BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config'\n    17→import Stripe from 'stripe'\n    18→\n    19→// Initialize Supabase client with service role key for webhook\n    20→const supabase = createClient(\n    21→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,\n    22→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!\n    23→)\n    24→\n    25→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    26→  const body = await req.text()\n    27→  const headersList = await headers()\n    28→  const signature = headersList.get('stripe-signature')\n    29→\n    30→  if (!signature) {\n    31→    return NextResponse.json(\n    32→      { error: 'Missing stripe-signature header' },\n    33→      { status: 400 }\n    34→    )\n    35→  }\n    36→\n    37→  let event: Stripe.Event\n    38→\n    39→  try {\n    40→    // Verify webhook signature\n    41→    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(\n    42→      body,\n    43→      signature,\n    44→      process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET!\n    45→    )\n    46→  } catch (err) {\n    47→    console.error('Webhook signature verification failed:', err)\n    48→    return NextResponse.json(\n    49→      { error: 'Webhook signature verification failed' },\n    50→      { status: 400 }\n    51→    )\n    52→  }\n    53→\n    54→  // Handle the event\n    55→  switch (event.type) {\n    56→    case 'checkout.session.completed':\n    57→      await handleCheckoutSessionCompleted(event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session)\n    58→      break\n    59→\n    60→    case 'customer.subscription.created':\n    61→    case 'customer.subscription.updated':\n    62→      await handleSubscriptionUpdate(event.data.object as Stripe.Subscription)\n    63→      break\n    64→\n    65→    case 'customer.subscription.deleted':\n    66→      await handleSubscriptionDeleted(event.data.object as Stripe.Subscription)\n    67→      break\n    68→\n    69→    case 'payment_intent.succeeded':\n    70→      console.log('PaymentIntent succeeded:', event.data.object.id)\n    71→      break\n    72→\n    73→    case 'payment_intent.payment_failed':\n    74→      console.log('PaymentIntent failed:', event.data.object.id)\n    75→      break\n    76→\n    77→    case 'invoice.payment_failed':\n    78→      await handleInvoicePaymentFailed(event.data.object as Stripe.Invoice)\n    79→      break\n    80→\n    81→    case 'charge.dispute.created':\n    82→      await handleChargeDisputeCreated(event.data.object as Stripe.Dispute)\n    83→      break\n    84→\n    85→    default:\n    86→      console.log(`Unhandled event type: ${event.type}`)\n    87→  }\n    88→\n    89→  return NextResponse.json({ received: true })\n    90→}\n    91→\n    92→/**\n    93→ * Handle checkout.session.completed event\n    94→ * Creates an order in the database when payment is successful\n    95→ */\n    96→async function handleCheckoutSessionCompleted(session: Stripe.Checkout.Session) {\n    97→  try {\n    98→     1→import { getTranslations } from 'next-intl/server'\n     2→import Link from 'next/link'\n     3→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'\n     4→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'\n     5→import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'\n     6→import { CheckCircle, Package } from 'lucide-react'\n     7→import { getCheckoutSession } from '@/lib/stripe-checkout'\n     8→import { LOCALE_FORMAT } from '@/lib/store-config'\n     9→import { CartClearer } from './CartClearer'\n    10→\n    11→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ locale: string }> }) {\n    12→  const { locale } = await params\n    13→  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: 'Checkout' })\n    14→\n    15→  return {\n    16→    title: t('successTitle'),\n    17→    description: t('successDescription'),\n    18→  }\n    19→}\n    20→\n    21→export default async function CheckoutSuccessPage({\n    22→  params,\n    23→  searchParams,\n    24→}: {\n    25→  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>\n    26→  searchParams: Promise<{ session_id?: string }>\n    27→}) {\n    28→  const { locale } = await params\n    29→  const { session_id } = await searchParams\n    30→  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: 'Checkout' })\n    31→\n    32→  // Fetch checkout session details from Stripe\n    33→  let sessionDetails = null\n    34→  if (session_id) {\n    35→    sessionDetails = await getCheckoutSession(session_id)\n    36→  }\n    37→\n    38→  // Format currency\n    39→  const formatCurrency = (amount: number, currency: string) => {\n    40→    return new Intl.NumberFormat(LOCALE_FORMAT[locale] || 'en-IE', {\n    41→      style: 'currency',\n    42→      currency: currency.toUpperCase(),\n    43→    }).format(amount / 100)\n    44→  }\n    45→\n    46→  return (\n    47→    <div className=\"min-h-[60vh] py-12 px-4\">\n    48→      {/* Clear cart after successful payment */}\n    49→      <CartClearer />\n    50→\n    51→      <div className=\"max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-8\">\n    52→        {/* Success Icon & Message */}\n    53→        <div className=\"text-center space-y-4\">\n    54→          <div className=\"flex justify-center\">\n    55→            <div className=\"size-20 rounded-full bg-success/10 flex items-center justify-center\">\n    56→              <CheckCircle className=\"size-12 text-success\" />\n    57→            </div>\n    58→          </div>\n    59→\n    60→          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n    61→            <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold text-foreground\">\n    62→              {t('successTitle')}\n    63→            </h1>\n    64→            <p className=\"text-muted-foreground\">\n    65→              {t('successDescription')}\n    66→            </p>\n    67→          </div>\n    68→        </div>\n    69→\n    70→        {/* Order Details Card */}\n    71→        {sessionDetails && sessionDetails.payment_status === 'paid' && (\n    72→          <Card>\n    73→            <CardHeader>\n    74→              <CardTitle className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n    75→                <Package className=\"size-5\" />\n    76→                {t('successOrderDetails')}\n    77→              </CardTitle>\n    78→            </CardHeader>\n    79→            <CardContent className=\"space-y-4\">\n    80→              {/* Customer Email */}\n    81→              {sessionDetails.customer_email && (\n    82→                <div>\n    83→                  <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">{t('successConfirmationSentTo')}</p>\n    84→                  <p className=\"font-medium\">{sessionDetails.customer_email}</p>\n    85→                </div>\n    86→              )}\n    87→\n    88→              <Separator />\n    89→\n    90→              {/* Line Items */}\n    91→              <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n    92→                <p className=\"text-sm font-medium\">{t('successItems')}</p>\n    93→                {sessionDetails.line_items.map((item, index) => (\n    94→                  <div key={index} className=\"flex justify-between text-sm\">\n    95→                    <div className=\"flex-1\">\n    96→                      <p className=\"font-medium\">{item.description}</p>\n    97→                      <p className=\"text-muted-foreground\">{t('successQuantityShort')} {item.quantity}</p>\n    98→                    </div>\n    99→                    <p className=\"font-medium\">\n   100→                      {formatCurrency(item.amount_total, sessionDetails.currency)}\n   101→                    </p>\n   102→                  </div>\n   103→                ))}\n   104→              </div>\n   105→\n   106→              <Separator />\n   107→\n   108→              {/* Total */}\n   109→              <div className=\"flex justify-between items-center\">\n   110→                <p className=\"font-semibold text-lg\">{t('successTotalPaid')}</p>\n   111→                <p className=\"font-bold text-2xl text-success\">\n   112→                  {formatCurrency(sessionDetails.amount_total, sessionDetails.currency)}\n   113→                </p>\n   114→              </div>\n   115→\n   116→              {/* Order Status */}\n   117→              <div className=\"bg-muted/50 p-4 rounded-lg\">\n   118→                <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n   119→                  {t('successPaymentStatus')} <span className=\"font-medium text-success capitalize\">{sessionDetails.payment_status}</span>\n   120→                </p>\n   121→              </div>\n   122→            </CardContent>\n   123→          </Card>\n   124→        )}\n   125→\n   126→        {/* No Session Details */}\n   127→        {!sessionDetails && session_id && (\n   128→          <Card>\n   129→            <CardContent className=\"py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground\">\n   130→              <p>{t('successUnableToLoad')}</p>\n   131→            </CardContent>\n   132→          </Card>\n   133→        )}\n   134→\n   135→        {/* Action Buttons */}\n   136→        <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center\">\n   137→          <Button asChild size=\"lg\" variant=\"outline\">\n   138→            <Link href={`/${locale}/shop`}>\n   139→              {t('successContinueShopping')}\n   140→            </Link>\n   141→          </Button>\n   142→          <Button asChild size=\"lg\">\n   143→            <Link href={`/${locale}/orders`}>\n   144→              {t('successViewOrders')}\n   145→            </Link>\n   146→          </Button>\n   147→        </div>\n   148→      </div>\n   100→    // Only process paid sessions\n   101→    if (session.payment_status !== 'paid') {\n   102→      console.log('Session not paid yet:', session.id)\n   103→      return\n   104→    }\n   105→\n   106→    // Extract metadata\n   107→    const locale = session.metadata?.locale || 'en'\n   108→    const cartItemsStr = session.metadata?.cart_items || '[]'\n   109→    const cartItems = JSON.parse(cartItemsStr)\n   110→    const giftMessage = session.metadata?.gift_message || null\n   111→\n   112→    // Get session details with line items and payment method\n   113→    const fullSession = await stripe.checkout.sessions.retrieve(session.id, {\n   114→      expand: ['line_items', 'payment_intent', 'payment_intent.payment_method'],\n   115→    })\n   116→\n   117→    const lineItems = fullSession.line_items?.data || []\n   118→\n   119→    // Get customer email\n   120→    const customerEmail = session.customer_details?.email || session.customer_email\n   121→\n   122→    // Build shipping address JSONB\n   123→    const shipping = session.collected_information?.shipping_details\n   124→    const shippingAddress = shipping?.address\n   125→      ? {\n   126→          name: shipping.name,\n   127→          line1: shipping.address.line1,\n   128→          line2: shipping.address.line2,\n   129→          city: shipping.address.city,\n   130→          state: shipping.address.state,\n   131→          postal_code: shipping.address.postal_code,\n   132→          country: shipping.address.country,\n   133→        }\n   134→      : null\n   135→\n   136→    // Get payment intent ID and payment method\n   137→    const paymentIntentId =\n   138→      typeof fullSession.payment_intent === 'string'\n   139→        ? fullSession.payment_intent\n   140→        : fullSession.payment_intent?.id\n   141→\n   142→    // Get payment method type (card, crypto, etc.)\n   143→    let paymentMethodType: string | null = null\n   144→    if (typeof fullSession.payment_intent !== 'string' && fullSession.payment_intent?.payment_method) {\n   145→      const paymentMethod = fullSession.payment_intent.payment_method\n   146→      if (typeof paymentMethod !== 'string') {\n   147→        paymentMethodType = paymentMethod.type || null\n   148→      }\n   149→    }\n   150→\n   151→    // Idempotency check: Check if order already exists for this session\n   152→    const { data: existingOrder } = await supabase\n   153→      .from('orders')\n   154→      .select('id, status, created_at')\n   155→      .eq('stripe_session_id', session.id)\n   156→      .single()\n   157→\n   158→    if (existingOrder) {\n   159→      console.log('Order already exists for session:', session.id, '— skipping (idempotent)')\n   160→      console.log('Existing order ID:', existingOrder.id)\n   161→      return // Idempotent: order already processed\n   162→    }\n   163→\n   164→    // Look up user by email (for authenticated users)\n   165→    let userId: string | null = null\n   166→    if (customerEmail) {\n   167→      const { data: userByEmail } = await supabase\n   168→        .from('users')\n   169→        .select('id')\n   170→        .eq('email', customerEmail)\n   171→        .single()\n   172→      userId = userByEmail?.id || null\n   173→    }\n   174→\n   175→    // Create order record\n   176→    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase\n   177→      .from('orders')\n   178→      .insert({\n   179→        user_id: userId,\n   180→        stripe_session_id: session.id,\n   181→        stripe_payment_intent_id: paymentIntentId,\n   182→        status: 'paid',\n   183→        total_cents: session.amount_total || 0,\n   184→        currency: session.currency || 'eur',\n   185→        shipping_address: shippingAddress,\n   186→        customer_email: customerEmail,\n   187→        locale,\n   188→        gift_message: giftMessage,\n   189→        payment_method: paymentMethodType,\n   190→        paid_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   191→      })\n   192→      .select()\n   193→      .single()\n   194→\n   195→    if (orderError) {\n   196→      console.error('Failed to create order:', orderError)\n   197→      throw orderError\n   198→    }\n   199→\n   200→    console.log('Created order:', order.id)\n   201→\n   202→    // Create order items (variant_id is required — NOT NULL constraint)\n   203→    const orderItems = lineItems.map((item, index) => {\n   204→      const cartItem = cartItems[index] || {}\n   205→      return {\n   206→        order_id: order.id,\n   207→        product_id: cartItem.product_id || null,\n   208→        variant_id: cartItem.variant_id,\n   209→        personalization_id: cartItem.personalization_id || null,\n   210→        composition_id: cartItem.composition_id || null,\n   211→        quantity: item.quantity || 1,\n   212→        unit_price_cents: Math.round((item.amount_total || 0) / (item.quantity || 1)),\n   213→      }\n   214→    })\n   215→\n   216→    // Filter out items without product_id or variant_id (data integrity check)\n   217→    const validOrderItems = orderItems.filter((item) => item.product_id && item.variant_id)\n   218→\n   219→    if (validOrderItems.length > 0) {\n   220→      const { error: itemsError } = await supabase\n   221→        .from('order_items')\n   222→        .insert(validOrderItems)\n   223→\n   224→      if (itemsError) {\n   225→        console.error('Failed to create order items:', itemsError)\n   226→        // Don't throw - order is already created\n   227→      } else {\n   228→        console.log(`Created ${validOrderItems.length} order items`)\n   229→      }\n   230→    }\n   231→\n   232→    // Create notification for the user (if user_id is found)\n   233→    // For guest checkouts, we'll create a notification linked to the email\n   234→    // Note: In a real system, you'd look up user by email first\n   235→    const orderDisplayId = order.id.slice(0, 8) // Use first 8 chars of UUID as display ID\n   236→    const itemCount = validOrderItems.length\n   237→    const totalAmount = (order.total_cents / 100).toFixed(2)\n   238→    const currencyCode = order.currency.toUpperCase()\n   239→\n   240→    const notificationData = {\n   241→      type: 'order_confirmation',\n   242→      title: `Order #${orderDisplayId} Confirmed`,\n   243→      body: `Your order has been confirmed. ${itemCount} ${itemCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} • Total: ${totalAmount} ${currencyCode}`,\n   244→      data: {\n   245→        order_id: order.id,\n   246→        session_id: session.id,\n   247→        total_cents: order.total_cents,\n   248→        currency: order.currency,\n   249→        item_count: itemCount,\n   250→      },\n   251→      is_read: false,\n   252→    }\n   253→\n   254→    // Create notification for authenticated user (reuse userId from earlier lookup)\n   255→    if (userId) {\n   256→      const { error: notifError } = await supabase\n   257→        .from('notifications')\n   258→        .insert({\n   259→          user_id: userId,\n   260→          ...notificationData,\n   261→        })\n   262→\n   263→      if (notifError) {\n   264→        console.error('Failed to create notification:', notifError)\n   265→      } else {\n   266→        console.log('Created notification for user:', userId)\n   267→      }\n   268→    } else {\n   269→      console.log('No user found for email - skipping notification (guest checkout)')\n   270→    }\n   271→\n   272→    // Create audit log entry\n   273→    const { error: auditError } = await supabase\n   274→      .from('audit_log')\n   275→      .insert({\n   276→        actor_type: 'webhook',\n   277→        actor_id: 'stripe_webhook',\n   278→        action: 'order_created',\n   279→        resource_type: 'order',\n   280→        resource_id: order.id,\n   281→        changes: {\n   282→          status: 'paid',\n   283→          total_cents: order.total_cents,\n   284→          currency: order.currency,\n   285→        },\n   286→        metadata: {\n   287→          stripe_session_id: session.id,\n   288→          stripe_payment_intent_id: paymentIntentId,\n   289→          customer_email: customerEmail,\n   290→          locale,\n   291→        },\n   292→      })\n   293→\n   294→    if (auditError) {\n   295→      console.error('Failed to create audit log entry:', auditError)\n   296→    } else {\n   297→      console.log('Created audit log entry for order:', order.id)\n   298→    }\n   299→\n   300→    // Increment coupon usage counter if a coupon was applied\n   301→    const couponCode = session.metadata?.coupon_code\n   302→    if (couponCode) {\n   303→      const { data: coupon } = await supabase\n   304→        .from('coupons')\n   305→        .select('id, times_used')\n   306→        .eq('code', couponCode)\n   307→        .single()\n   308→\n   309→      if (coupon) {\n   310→        const { error: couponError } = await supabase\n   311→          .from('coupons')\n   312→          .update({ times_used: (coupon.times_used || 0) + 1 })\n   313→          .eq('id', coupon.id)\n   314→\n   315→        if (couponError) {\n   316→          console.error('Failed to increment coupon usage:', couponError)\n   317→        } else {\n   318→          console.log(`Incremented coupon \"${couponCode}\" usage to ${(coupon.times_used || 0) + 1}`)\n   319→        }\n   320→      }\n   321→    }\n   322→\n   323→    // Submit order to POD provider\n   324→    if (validOrderItems.length > 0 && shippingAddress && customerEmail) {\n   325→      try {\n   326→        initializeProviders()\n   327→        const provider = getProvider()\n   328→        console.log('Submitting order to POD provider...')\n   329→\n   330→        // Fetch product and variant provider IDs\n   331→        const productIds = validOrderItems.map(item => item.product_id)\n   332→        const variantIds = validOrderItems.map(item => item.variant_id).filter(Boolean)\n   333→\n   334→        const { data: products } = await supabase\n   335→          .from('products')\n   336→          .select('id, provider_product_id')\n   337→          .in('id', productIds)\n   338→\n   339→        const { data: variants } = await supabase\n   340→          .from('product_variants')\n   341→          .select('id, external_variant_id')\n   342→          .in('id', variantIds)\n   343→\n   344→        // Create lookup maps\n   345→        const productMap = new Map(products?.map(p => [p.id, p.provider_product_id]) || [])\n   346→        const variantMap = new Map(variants?.map(v => [v.id, v.external_variant_id]) || [])\n   347→\n   348→        // Guard: verify all items have valid provider variant mappings\n   349→        const itemsMissingProviderVariant = validOrderItems.filter(item => {\n   350→          const pvId = variantMap.get(item.variant_id)\n   351→          return !pvId || pvId === '0'\n   352→        })\n   353→\n   354→        if (itemsMissingProviderVariant.length > 0) {\n   355→          console.error('Order items missing provider variant mapping:', itemsMissingProviderVariant)\n   356→          await supabase.from('orders').update({\n   357→            pod_error: 'Items missing provider variant mapping',\n   358→            status: 'requires_review',\n   359→          }).eq('id', order.id)\n   360→\n   361→          await notifyAdminOfProviderFailure(order.id, 'variant_mapping', 'One or more items have no provider variant mapping')\n   362→          if (customerEmail) {\n   363→            await sendOrderIssueEmail(customerEmail, order.id, order.locale || 'en')\n   364→          }\n   365→          return\n   366→        }\n   367→\n   368→        // Build production URLs map from cart metadata (keyed by composition_id)\n   369→        const productionUrlsMap = new Map<string, Record<string, string>>()\n   370→        for (const ci of cartItems) {\n   371→          if (ci.composition_id && ci.production_urls) {\n   372→            productionUrlsMap.set(ci.composition_id, ci.production_urls)\n   373→          }\n   374→        }\n   375→\n   376→        // Build provider line items (variant_id is guaranteed non-null)\n   377→        const providerLineItems = validOrderItems\n   378→          .filter(item => {\n   379→            const providerProductId = dispute_currency: dispute.currency,\n   945→        },\n   946→      })\n   947→\n   948→    // Send admin alert notification\n   949→    const { data: admins } = await supabase\n   950→     1→/**\n     2→ * Tax Calculation API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/checkout/calculate-tax\n     5→ * Calculates tax for a cart using Stripe Tax\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';\n     9→import { calculateTax } from '@/lib/stripe';\n    10→import { STORE_DEFAULTS } from '@/lib/store-config';\n    11→\n    12→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    13→  try {\n    14→    const body = await req.json();\n    15→    const { cartItems, shippingAddress, currency = STORE_DEFAULTS.stripeCurrency, shipping } = body;\n    16→\n    17→    // Validate required fields\n    18→    if (!cartItems || !Array.isArray(cartItems) || cartItems.length === 0) {\n    19→      return NextResponse.json(\n    20→        { error: 'Cart items are required' },\n    21→        { status: 400 }\n    22→      );\n    23→    }\n    24→\n    25→    if (!shippingAddress) {\n    26→      return NextResponse.json(\n    27→        { error: 'Shipping address is required' },\n    28→        { status: 400 }\n    29→      );\n    30→    }\n    31→\n    32→    // Validate shipping address fields\n    33→    const requiredFields = ['line1', 'city', 'state', 'postal_code', 'country'];\n    34→    for (const field of requiredFields) {\n    35→      if (!shippingAddress[field]) {\n    36→        return NextResponse.json(\n    37→          { error: `Shipping address ${field} is required` },\n    38→          { status: 400 }\n    39→        );\n    40→      }\n    41→    }\n    42→\n    43→    // Calculate tax using Stripe Tax\n    44→    const result = await calculateTax({\n    45→      cartItems,\n    46→      shippingAddress,\n    47→      currency,\n    48→      shipping,\n    49→    });\n    50→\n    51→    return NextResponse.json({\n    52→      success: true,\n    53→      tax: result.tax,\n    54→      total: result.total,\n    55→      breakdown: result.breakdown,\n    56→    });\n    57→  } catch (error) {\n    58→    console.error('Tax calculation error:', error);\n    59→    return NextResponse.json(\n    60→      { error: 'Failed to calculate tax' },\n    61→      { status: 500 }\n    62→    );\n    63→  }\n    64→}\n    65→"}]},"uuid":"2a24e055-d803-41ee-8b0a-e3a92717d033","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:31.362Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"25cccc99-b787-4078-8315-2a7838fd0c85"}

{"parentUuid":"d248b667-3d3b-41e0-b44c-ed07d454fd1d","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"ab3d21b156434cfd0","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016avxLzV7jkytLffJb64t38","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import { useState } from 'react'\n     4→import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'\n     5→import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'\n     6→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'\n     7→import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'\n     8→import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'\n     9→import { X } from 'lucide-react'\n    10→import { LOCALE_COUNTRY, STORE_DEFAULTS } from '@/lib/store-config'\n    11→\n    12→interface AddressFormProps {\n    13→  onSubmit: (address: AddressFormData) => Promise<void>\n    14→  onCancel: () => void\n    15→}\n    16→\n    17→export interface AddressFormData {\n    18→  label: string\n    19→  full_name: string\n    20→  street_address: string\n    21→  street_address_2?: string\n    22→  city: string\n    23→  state: string\n    24→  postal_code: string\n    25→  country_code: string\n    26→  phone?: string\n    27→  is_default: boolean\n    28→}\n    29→\n    30→interface FormErrors {\n    31→  label?: string\n    32→  full_name?: string\n    33→  street_address?: string\n    34→  city?: string\n    35→  state?: string\n    36→  postal_code?: string\n    37→  country_code?: string\n    38→}\n    39→\n    40→export default function AddressForm({ onSubmit, onCancel }: AddressFormProps) {\n    41→  const t = useTranslations('Checkout')\n    42→  const params = useParams()\n    43→  const locale = (params?.locale as string) || 'en'\n    44→\n    45→  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<AddressFormData>({\n    46→    label: '',\n    47→    full_name: '',\n    48→    street_address: '',\n    49→    street_address_2: '',\n    50→    city: '',\n    51→    state: '',\n    52→    postal_code: '',\n    53→    country_code: LOCALE_COUNTRY[locale] || STORE_DEFAULTS.country,\n    54→    phone: '',\n    55→    is_default: false,\n    56→  })\n    57→\n    58→  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({})\n    59→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\n    60→\n    61→  const validateForm = (): boolean => {\n    62→    const newErrors: FormErrors = {}\n    63→\n    64→    if (!formData.label.trim()) {\n    65→      newErrors.label = t('validation.labelRequired')\n    66→    }\n    67→    if (!formData.full_name.trim()) {\n    68→      newErrors.full_name = t('validation.fullNameRequired')\n    69→    }\n    70→    if (!formData.street_address.trim()) {\n    71→      newErrors.street_address = t('validation.streetAddressRequired')\n    72→    }\n    73→    if (!formData.city.trim()) {\n    74→      newErrors.city = t('validation.cityRequired')\n    75→    }\n    76→    if (!formData.state.trim()) {\n    77→      newErrors.state = t('validation.stateRequired')\n    78→    }\n    79→    if (!formData.postal_code.trim()) {\n    80→      newErrors.postal_code = t('validation.postalCodeRequired')\n    81→    }\n    82→    if (!formData.country_code.trim()) {\n    83→      newErrors.country_code = t('validation.countryRequired')\n    84→    }\n    85→\n    86→    setErrors(newErrors)\n    87→    return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0\n    88→  }\n    89→\n    90→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    91→    e.preventDefault()\n    92→\n    93→    if (!validateForm()) {\n    94→      return\n    95→    }\n    96→\n    97→    setLoading(true)\n    98→    try {\n    99→      await onSubmit(formData)\n   100→    } catch (error) {\n   101→      console.error('Error submitting address:', error)\n   102→    } finally {\n   103→      setLoading(false)\n   104→    }\n   105→  }\n   106→\n   107→  const handleChange = (field: keyof AddressFormData, value: string | boolean) => {\n   108→    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }))\n   109→    // Clear error for this field when user types\n   110→    if (errors[field as keyof FormErrors]) {\n   111→      setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: undefined }))\n   112→    }\n   113→  }\n   114→\n   115→  return (\n   116→    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4 p-4 border-2 border-primary rounded-lg bg-primary/5\">\n   117→      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-4\">\n   118→        <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-foreground\">{t('newAddress')}</h3>\n   119→        <Button\n   120→          type=\"button\"\n   121→          variant=\"ghost\"\n   122→          size=\"sm\"\n   123→          onClick={onCancel}\n   124→          className=\"size-8 p-0\"\n   125→        >\n   126→          <X className=\"size-4\" />\n   127→        </Button>\n   128→      </div>\n   129→\n   130→      {/* Label */}\n   131→      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   132→        <Label htmlFor=\"label\">\n   133→          {t('addressLabel')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   134→        </Label>\n   135→        <Input\n   136→          id=\"label\"\n   137→          value={formData.label}\n   138→          onChange={(e) => handleChange('label', e.target.value)}\n   139→          placeholder={t('addressLabelPlaceholder')}\n   140→          className={errors.label ? 'border-destructive' : ''}\n   141→        />\n   142→        {errors.label && (\n   143→          <p className=\"text-sm text-destructive\">{errors.label}</p>\n   144→        )}\n   145→      </div>\n   146→\n   147→      {/* Full Name */}\n   148→      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   149→        <Label htmlFor=\"full_name\">\n   150→          {t('fullName')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   151→        </Label>\n   152→        <Input\n   153→          id=\"full_name\"\n   154→          autoComplete=\"name\"\n   155→          value={formData.full_name}\n   156→          onChange={(e) => handleChange('full_name', e.target.value)}\n   157→          placeholder={t('fullNamePlaceholder')}\n   158→          className={errors.full_name ? 'border-destructive' : ''}\n   159→        />\n   160→        {errors.full_name && (\n   161→          <p className=\"text-sm text-destructive\">{errors.full_name}</p>\n   162→        )}\n   163→      </div>\n   164→\n   165→      {/* Street Address */}\n   166→      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   167→        <Label htmlFor=\"street_address\">\n   168→          {t('streetAddress')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   169→        </Label>\n   170→        <Input\n   171→          id=\"street_address\"\n   172→          autoComplete=\"street-address\"\n   173→          value={formData.street_address}\n   174→          onChange={(e) => handleChange('street_address', e.target.value)}\n   175→          placeholder={t('streetAddressPlaceholder')}\n   176→          className={errors.street_address ? 'border-destructive' : ''}\n   177→        />\n   178→        {errors.street_address && (\n   179→          <p className=\"text-sm text-destructive\">{errors.street_address}</p>\n   180→        )}\n   181→      </div>\n   182→\n   183→      {/* Street Address 2 (Optional) */}\n   184→      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   185→        <Label htmlFor=\"street_address_2\">{t('streetAddress2')}</Label>\n   186→        <Input\n   187→          id=\"street_address_2\"\n   188→          autoComplete=\"address-line2\"\n   189→          value={formData.street_address_2}\n   190→          onChange={(e) => handleChange('street_address_2', e.target.value)}\n   191→          placeholder={t('streetAddress2Placeholder')}\n   192→        />\n   193→      </div>\n   194→\n   195→      {/* City and State */}\n   196→      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n   197→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   198→          <Label htmlFor=\"city\">\n   199→            {t('city')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   200→          </Label>\n   201→          <Input\n   202→            id=\"city\"\n   203→            autoComplete=\"address-level2\"\n   204→            value={formData.city}\n   205→            onChange={(e) => handleChange('city', e.target.value)}\n   206→            placeholder={t('cityPlaceholder')}\n   207→            className={errors.city ? 'border-destructive' : ''}\n   208→          />\n   209→          {errors.city && (\n   210→            <p className=\"text-sm text-destructive\">{errors.city}</p>\n   211→          )}\n   212→        </div>\n   213→\n   214→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   215→          <Label htmlFor=\"state\">\n   216→            {t('state')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   217→          </Label>\n   218→          <Input\n   219→            id=\"state\"\n   220→            autoComplete=\"address-level1\"\n   221→            value={formData.state}\n   222→            onChange={(e) => handleChange('state', e.target.value)}\n   223→            placeholder={t('statePlaceholder')}\n   224→            className={errors.state ? 'border-destructive' : ''}\n   225→          />\n   226→          {errors.state && (\n   227→            <p className=\"text-sm text-destructive\">{errors.state}</p>\n   228→          )}\n   229→        </div>\n   230→      </div>\n   231→\n   232→      {/* Postal Code and Country */}\n   233→      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n   234→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   235→          <Label htmlFor=\"postal_code\">\n   236→            {t('postalCode')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   237→          </Label>\n   238→          <Input\n   239→            id=\"postal_code\"\n   240→            autoComplete=\"postal-code\"\n   241→            inputMode=\"numeric\"\n   242→            value={formData.postal_code}\n   243→            onChange={(e) => handleChange('postal_code', e.target.value)}\n   244→            placeholder={t('postalCodePlaceholder')}\n   245→            className={errors.postal_code ? 'border-destructive' : ''}\n   246→          />\n   247→          {errors.postal_code && (\n   248→            <p className=\"text-sm text-destructive\">{errors.postal_code}</p>\n   249→          )}\n   250→        </div>\n   251→\n   252→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   253→          <Label htmlFor=\"country_code\">\n   254→            {t('country')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   255→          </Label>\n   256→          <Input\n   257→            id=\"country_code\"\n   258→            autoComplete=\"country\"\n   259→            value={formData.country_code}\n   260→            onChange={(e) => handleChange('country_code', e.target.value)}\n   261→            placeholder={t('countryPlaceholder')}\n   262→            className={errors.country_code ? {"parentUuid":"99c92e9c-e51d-4652-b7c2-5c2d58ef4151","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"ab3d21b156434cfd0","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TWs1Cw999rNsKFoadqVp4f","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Orders API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * GET /api/orders\n     5→ * Retrieves the authenticated user's order history\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     9→import { requireAuth, authErrorResponse } from '@/lib/auth-guard'\n    10→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    11→\n    12→const supabase = createClient(\n    13→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,\n    14→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!\n    15→)\n    16→\n    17→export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n    18→  try {\n    19→    let user\n    20→    try {\n    21→      user = await requireAuth(req)\n    22→    } catch (error) {\n    23→      return authErrorResponse(error)\n    24→    }\n    25→\n    26→    // Parse pagination parameters\n    27→    const searchParams = req.nextUrl.searchParams\n    28→    const page = parseInt(searchParams.get('page') || '1')\n    29→    const limit = Math.min(parseInt(searchParams.get('limit') || '10'), 100) // Max 100 per page\n    30→    const status = searchParams.get('status') // Optional status filter\n    31→\n    32→    // Calculate offset for pagination\n    33→    const offset = (page - 1) * limit\n    34→\n    35→    // Multi-tenant isolation: filter by tenant_id when x-tenant-id header is set\n    36→    const tenantId = req.headers.get('x-tenant-id') || null\n    37→\n    38→    // Build query with pagination\n    39→    let query = supabase\n    40→      .from('orders')\n    41→      .select('id, status, total_cents, currency, created_at, paid_at, shipped_at, tracking_number, customer_email', { count: 'exact' })\n    42→      .eq('user_id', user.id)\n    43→      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n    44→      .range(offset, offset + limit - 1)\n    45→\n    46→    // Apply tenant isolation when header is present\n    47→    if (tenantId) {\n    48→      query = query.eq('tenant_id', tenantId)\n    49→    }\n    50→\n    51→    // Apply status filter if provided\n    52→    if (status) {\n    53→      // Support comma-separated statuses (e.g., \"pending,processing\")\n    54→      const statuses = status.split(',').map(s => s.trim())\n    55→      query = query.in('status', statuses)\n    56→    }\n    57→\n    58→    const { data: orders, error, count } = await query\n    59→\n    60→    if (error) {\n    61→      console.error('Error fetching orders:', error)\n    62→      return NextResponse.json(\n    63→        { error: 'Failed to fetch orders' },\n    64→        { status: 500 }\n    65→      )\n    66→    }\n    67→\n    68→    // Calculate pagination metadata\n    69→    const total = count || 0\n    70→    const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / limit)\n    71→\n    72→    return NextResponse.json({\n    73→      orders: orders || [],\n    74→      pagination: {\n    75→        page,\n    76→        limit,\n    77→        total,\n    78→        totalPages,\n    79→        hasNextPage: page < totalPages,\n    80→        hasPreviousPage: page > 1,\n    81→      },\n    82→    })\n    83→  } catch (error) {\n    84→    console.error('Orders API error:', error)\n    85→    return NextResponse.json(\n    86→      { error: 'Internal server error' },\n    87→      { status: 500 }\n    88→    )\n    89→  }\n    90→}\n    91→"}]},"uuid":"a5430928-47eb-43d2-b528-45a80e59a874","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:31.810Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"99c92e9c-e51d-4652-b7c2-5c2d58ef4151"}

Extract provider from URL path\n     9→ * 2. Read raw body\n    10→ * 3. Initialize and lookup provider\n    11→ * 4. Verify webhook signature (HMAC for Printify, query secret for Printful)\n    12→ * 5. Normalize event to canonical format\n    13→ * 6. Write audit log\n    14→ * 7. Route to appropriate handler\n    15→ * 8. Always return 200 after verification (prevents provider retries)\n    16→ */\n    17→\n    18→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n    19→import { initializeProviders, getProviderById } from '@/lib/pod'\n    20→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n    21→import { createWebhookRouter } from '@/lib/pod/webhooks'\n    22→\n    23→// Create a singleton router (all handlers are stateless, safe to reuse)\n    24→const webhookRouter = createWebhookRouter()\n    25→\n    26→/** Map of provider IDs to their webhook signature header names */\n    27→const SIGNATURE_HEADERS: Record<string, string> = {\n    28→  printify: 'x-printify-hmac-sha256',\n    29→  printful: '', // Printful uses query param ?secret=, not a header\n    30→}\n    31→\n    32→/** Known provider IDs */\n    33→const KNOWN_PROVIDERS = new Set(['printify', 'printful'])\n    34→\n    35→export async function POST(\n    36→  req: NextRequest,\n    37→  { params }: { params: Promise<{ provider: string }> },\n    38→) {\n    39→  const { provider: providerId } = await params\n    40→\n    41→  // Validate provider ID\n    42→  if (!KNOWN_PROVIDERS.has(providerId)) {\n    43→    return NextResponse.json(\n    44→      { error: `Unknown provider: ${providerId}` },\n    45→      { status: 404 },\n    46→    )\n    47→  }\n    48→\n    49→  // Read raw body for signature verification\n    50→  const body = await req.text()\n    51→\n    52→  // Initialize all configured providers\n    53→  initializeProviders()\n    54→\n    55→  // Get the provider instance\n    56→  let providerInstance\n    57→  try {\n    58→    providerInstance = getProviderById(providerId)\n    59→  } catch {\n    60→    console.error(`[webhook:${providerId}] Provider not configured`)\n    61→    return NextResponse.json(\n    62→      { error: `Provider ${providerId} is not configured` },\n    63→      { status: 500 },\n    64→    )\n    65→  }\n    66→\n    67→  // Extract signature based on provider type\n    68→  let signature: string\n    69→  if (providerId === 'printful') {\n    70→    // Printful uses ?secret= query parameter\n    71→    signature = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get('secret') || ''\n    72→  } else {\n    73→    // Printify and others use header-based signatures\n    74→    const headerName = SIGNATURE_HEADERS[providerId] || ''\n    75→    signature = req.headers.get(headerName) || ''\n    76→  }\n    77→\n    78→  if (!signature) {\n    79→    console.error(`[webhook:${providerId}] Missing webhook signature`)\n    80→    return NextResponse.json(\n    81→      { error: 'Missing webhook signature' },\n    82→      { status: 401 },\n    83→    )\n    84→  }\n    85→\n    86→  // Verify the webhook signature\n    87→  const isValid = providerInstance.verifyWebhook(body, signature)\n    88→  if (!isValid) {\n    89→    console.error(`[webhook:${providerId}] Webhook signature verification failed`)\n    90→    return NextResponse.json(\n    91→      { error: 'Invalid signature' },\n    92→      { status: 401 },\n    93→    )\n    94→  }\n    95→\n    96→  // Parse and normalize the event\n    97→  let rawEvent: unknown\n    98→  try {\n    99→    rawEvent = JSON.parse(body)\n   100→  } catch {\n   101→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid JSON body' }, { status: 400 })\n   102→  }\n   103→\n   104→  let event\n   105→  try {\n   106→    event = providerInstance.normalizeEvent(rawEvent)\n   107→  } catch (error) {\n   108→    console.error(`[webhook:${providerId}] Failed to normalize event:`, error)\n   109→    return NextResponse.json(\n   110→      { error: 'Failed to normalize event' },\n   111→      { status: 400 },\n   112→    )\n   113→  }\n   114→\n   115→  // Write audit log entry for the raw event\n   116→  try {\n   117→    await supabaseAdmin.from('audit_log').insert({\n   118→      actor_type: 'webhook',\n   119→      actor_id: `${providerId}_webhook`,\n   120→      action: `webhook_received:${event.type}`,\n   121→      resource_type: 'webhook',\n   122→      resource_id: event.eventId || event.resourceId || 'unknown',\n   123→      changes: { normalized_type: event.type },\n   124→      metadata: {\n   125→        provider: providerId,\n   126→        resource_id: event.resourceId,\n   127→        event_id: event.eventId,\n   128→      },\n   129→    })\n   130→  } catch (auditError) {\n   131→    // Audit log failure is not critical — continue processing\n   132→    console.error(`[webhook:${providerId}] Failed to write audit log:`, auditError)\n   133→  }\n   134→\n   135→  // Route the event to its handler\n   136→  try {\n   137→    await webhookRouter.route(event, supabaseAdmin)\n   138→  } catch (error) {\n   139→    console.error(`[webhook:${providerId}] Error handling event ${event.type}:`, error)\n   140→    // Return 200 to prevent provider from retrying — error is logged\n   141→    return NextResponse.json({ received: true, error: 'Processing error' })\n   142→  }\n   143→\n   144→  return NextResponse.json({ received: true })\n   145→}\n value.bind(client) : value\n    44→  },\n    45→})\n    46→\n    47→/**\n    48→ * Send order confirmation email\n    49→ */\n    50→export async function sendOrderConfirmationEmail(params: {\n    51→  to: string\n    52→  orderId: string\n    53→  orderNumber: string\n    54→  itemCount: number\n    55→  totalCents: number\n    56→  currency: string\n    57→  locale: string\n    58→}) {\n    59→  const { to, orderId, orderNumber, itemCount, totalCents, currency, locale } = params\n    60→\n    61→  const { brandName, brandTagline } = await getBrandConfig()\n    62→  const totalAmount = (totalCents / 100).toFixed(2)\n    63→  const currencyCode = currency.toUpperCase()\n    64→\n    65→  // Locale-aware email content\n    66→  const subjects = {\n    67→    en: `Order Confirmation #${orderNumber}`,\n    68→    es: `Confirmación de Pedido #${orderNumber}`,\n    69→    de: `Bestellbestätigung #${orderNumber}`,\n    70→  }\n    71→\n    72→  const headings = {\n    73→    en: 'Thank you for your order! 🎉',\n    74→    es: '¡Gracias por tu pedido! 🎉',\n    75→    de: 'Vielen Dank für deine Bestellung! 🎉',\n    76→  }\n    77→\n    78→  const bodies = {\n    79→    en: `Your order has been confirmed and is being processed. You will receive another email when your order ships.`,\n    80→    es: `Tu pedido ha sido confirmado y está siendo procesado. 8→export interface CheckoutSessionDetails {\n     9→  id: string\n    10→  status: string\n    11→  payment_status: string\n    12→  customer_email: string | null\n    13→  amount_total: number\n    14→  currency: string\n    15→  line_items: Array<{\n    16→    description: string\n    17→    quantity: number\n    18→    amount_total: number\n    19→  }>\n    20→  metadata: {\n    21→    locale?: string\n    22→    cart_items?: string\n    23→  }\n    24→}\n    25→\n    26→/**\n    27→ * Retrieve checkout session details from Stripe\n    28→ */\n    29→export async function getCheckoutSession(\n    30→  sessionId: string\n    31→): Promise<CheckoutSessionDetails | null> {\n    32→  try {\n    33→    if (!sessionId || !sessionId.startsWith('cs_')) {\n    34→      console.error('Invalid Stripe session ID format:', sessionId?.slice(0, 10))\n    35→      return null\n    36→    }\n    37→\n    38→    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.retrieve(sessionId, {\n    39→      expand: ['line_items'],\n    40→    })\n    41→\n    42→    if (!session) {\n    43→      return null\n    44→    }\n    45→\n    46→    const lineItems = session.line_items?.data.map((item) => ({\n    47→      description: item.description || '',\n    48→      quantity: item.quantity || 0,\n    49→      amount_total: item.amount_total || 0,\n    50→    })) || []\n    51→\n    52→    return {\n    53→      id: session.id,\n    54→      status: session.status || 'unknown',\n    55→      payment_status: session.payment_status || 'unknown',\n    56→      customer_email: session.customer_details?.email || null,\n    57→      amount_total: session.amount_total || 0,\n    58→      currency: session.currency || 'eur',\n    59→      line_items: lineItems,\n    60→      metadata: session.metadata || {},\n    61→    }\n    62→  } catch (error) {\n    63→    console.error('Failed to retrieve checkout session:', error)\n    64→    return null\n    65→  }\n    66→}\n    67→"}]},"uuid":"0b977284-8c7b-41dd-8178-06665fbd44a9","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:46.896Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"74c2b35d-c980-4ac5-8097-a12d3d6476a5"}

CheckoutView Component Workflow**\n\n**Path:** `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/checkout/CheckoutView.tsx` (737 lines)\n\n**Flow Steps:**\n\n1. **Load Cart Items** (lines 44, 76-77)\n   - Uses `useCart()` hook to fetch cart items\n   - Calculates `cartTotal` and `itemCount`\n   - Shows loading skeleton or \"empty cart\" state if needed\n\n2. **Address Selection/Creation** (lines 80-105, 366-505)\n   - **Authenticated Users:** Fetch saved addresses via `GET /api/shipping-addresses` (line 90)\n   - Auto-select default address if available (lines 95-98)\n   - Allow adding new address via `AddressForm` component (lines 167-201)\n   - New addresses POSTed to `POST /api/shipping-addresses` (line 169)\n   - **Guest Users:** Email validation required (lines 204-221)\n   - Email regex validation: `/^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/` (line 205)\n\n3. **Tax Calculation** (lines 107-164)\n   - Triggered when `selectedAddressId` changes (line 164)\n   - POSTs to `POST /api/checkout/calculate-tax` (line 128)\n   - **Request Format:**\n     ```json\n     {\n       \"cartItems\": [{ productId, name, amount (cents), quantity }],\n       \"shippingAddress\": { line1, line2, city, state, postal_code, country },\n       \"currency\": \"eur\" (or user's currency)\n     }\n     ```\n   - **Response:** `{ tax (cents), total, breakdown }`\n   - Tax stored in state: `calculatedTax` (lines 49, 150)\n   - Displays as \"Calculating...\" while in progress (line 653)\n\n4. **Order Summary Panel** (lines 575-718)\n   - Sticky sidebar (right side on desktop)\n   - Displays items with image, size/color, quantity, price\n   - Shows subtotal, discount (if coupon applied), shipping, tax\n   - **Total calculation:** `discountedTotal + (calculatedTax || 0)` (line 673)\n   - Gift message toggle & textarea (lines 682-704)\n   - Coupon discount shown if applied (lines 638-642)\n\n5. **Payment Intent Creation** (lines 210-279)\n   - Validation: All required fields checked before submission\n   - POSTs to `POST /api/checkout/create-session` (line 247)\n   - **Request Body:**\n     ```typescript\n     {\n       cartItems,\n       locale,\n       currency: userCurrency.toLowerCase(),\n       customerEmail (guest checkout only),\n       gift_message (if enabled),\n       couponCode (if applied)\n     }\n     ```\n   - **Response:** `{ success, sessionId, url }`\n   - On success: `window.location.href = data.url` (redirects to Stripe Checkout) (line 259)\n   - **Error Handling:**\n     - 409 status: Item unavailable — shows toast with unavailable items (lines 261-268)\n     - Other errors: Generic \"payment error\" toast (line 271)\n\n#### **C. Address Form Component**\n\n**Path:** `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/checkout/AddressForm.tsx` (305 lines)\n\n- **Required Fields:** Label, full_name, street_address, city, state, postal_code, country_code\n- **Optional Fields:** street_address_2, phone, is_default\n- **Default Country:** Based on locale (line 53)\n- **Validation:** Client-side form validation with error display (lines 61-88)\n- **Submission:** Async submission to `POST /api/shipping-addresses` (lines 90-105)\n\n---\n\n### 2. STRIPE CHECKOUT SESSION CREATION API\n\n**Path:** `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/app/api/checkout/create-session/route.ts` (435 lines)\n\n#### **A. Rate Limiting**\n- **Rate Limiter:** `checkoutLimiter.check(ip)` (line 33)\n- **Limit:** 5 requests per minute per IP\n- **Response:** 429 if exceeded (line 35)\n\n#### **B. Validation**\n\n1. **Cart Items Validation** (lines 42-63)\n   - Must be non-empty array\n   - All items MUST have `variant_id` (required for Printful fulfillment) — 400 if missing\n   - Validates structure: `{ product_id, variant_id, quantity, variant_details?, composition_id? }`\n\n2. **Product Status Check** (lines 68-89)\n   - Queries `products` table: `status='active'` AND `deleted_at IS NULL`\n   - Returns 409 if any product is inactive\n   - Error code: `PRODUCTS_UNAVAILABLE`\n\n3. **Stock Validation** (lines 91-133)\n   - Queries `product_variants` for matching `color`, `size`, `is_enabled=true`\n   - Checks `is_available` flag per variant\n   - **Logic:** For each cart item, find matching variant by `(product_id, color, size)` tuple\n   - If no match OR `is_available=false`, add to `unavailableItems` array\n   - Returns 409 with unavailable items if any found\n\n#### **C. Coupon Validation** (lines 135-160)\n\n1. **Lookup:** Query `coupons` table by `code.toUpperCase()` (lines 138-143)\n2. **Eligibility Check:**\n   - Must be `active=true`\n   - Check `min_purchase_amount` (cart total must meet minimum)\n   - Check `usage_limit` vs `times_used` (if usage_limit set)\n3. **Valid Coupon:** Extract `discount_type` (percentage/fixed) and `discount_value`\n4. **Coupon Object:** Stored for later Stripe coupon creation\n\n#### **D. Composition (Custom Design) Resolution** (lines 162-187)\n\n- Lookup `design_compositions` table by `composition_id`\n- Fetch `production_url` (can be JSON for multi-panel or direct URL)\n- Parse & store in `item._production_urls` for later POD submission\n- Non-blocking: errors logged but don't fail checkout\n\n#### **E. Server-Side Price Authority** (lines 189-213)\n\n- **Override client prices** with DB variant prices\n- Query `product_variants` for all items' `price_cents`\n- Match by `(product_id, color, size)` tuple\n- Set `item.product_price = price_cents / 100`\n- **Reason:** Prevent price manipulation from client\n\n#### **F. Line Items Construction** (lines 215-269)\n\n1. **Build Stripe line items:** Iterate cart items (lines 216-239)\n   - `product_data`: { name, description (optional), images (if valid) }\n   - `unit_amount`: Convert euros to cents: `Math.round(price * 100)`\n   - `quantity`: From cart item\n\n2. **Coupon Discount Application** (lines 241-269)\n   - Calculate discount amount in cents:\n     - Percentage: `totalCents * (discount_value / 100)`\n     - Fixed: `discount_value * 100`\n   - Cap discount at cart total\n   - **Create Stripe Coupon:** `stripe.coupons.create()` with `amount_off` + `duration='once'`\n   - Store coupon ID in `lineItems._stripeCouponId` for session attachment\n\n#### **G. Shipping & Success/Cancel URLs** (lines 271-274)\n\n- **Success URL:** `{baseUrl}/{locale}/checkout/success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}`\n- **Cancel URL:** `{baseUrl}/{locale}/checkout/cancel`\n- **Shipping Threshold:** Free shipping if subtotal >= €50, else €4.99\n\n#### **H. Payment Method Types** (lines 276-280)\n\n- Always includes: `card`\n- Conditionally: `crypto` (if `STRIPE_CRYPTO_ENABLED='true'`)\n\n#### **I. Stripe Connect (Multi-Tenant)** (lines 282-332)\n\n- **Per-tenant routing:** Lookup `x-tenant-id` header\n- Query `tenant_configs` for `stripe:connected_account_id`\n- Query `tenants` for plan tier\n- **Plan fees:**\n  - Free: 10%\n  - Starter: 5%\n  - Pro: 3%\n  - Enterprise: 2%\n- **Session Config:** Add `payment_intent_data.transfer_data.destination` + `application_fee_amount`\n\n#### **J. Stripe Checkout Session Creation** (lines 342-418)\n\n**Session Config:**\n```typescript\n{\n  mode: 'payment',\n  payment_method_types: ['card', ...],\n  line_items: [...],\n  success_url,\n  cancel_url,\n  locale: (es|de|en),\n  currency: (eur|etc),\n  automatic_tax: { enabled: false },\n  shipping_address_collection: (only if no pre-filled address),\n  shipping_options: [\n    {\n      type: 'fixed_amount',\n      fixed_amount: { amount: (0 or 499), currency },\n      display_name: (Free Shipping|Standard Shipping €4.99),\n      delivery_estimate: { min: 5 days, max: 7 days }\n    }\n  ],\n  metadata: {\n    locale,\n    cart_items: JSON.stringify([...]), // with product_id, variant_id, quantity, composition_id, production_urls\n    gift_message (if enabled),\n    coupon_code (if applied)\n  },\n  customer_email (guest checkout),\n  discounts: [{ coupon: stripeCouponId }] (if coupon)\n}\n```\n\n**Response:** `{ success: true, sessionId, url }`\n\n---\n\n### 3. STRIPE WEBHOOK HANDLER\n\n**Path:** `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts` (1002 lines)\n\n#### **A. Webhook Signature Verification** (lines 25-52)\n\n- **Signature Header:** `stripe-signature`\n- **Verification:** `stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(body, signature, STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET)`\n- **Error:** Returns 400 if signature invalid\n\n#### **B. Event Types Handled**\n\n| Event Type | Handler Function | Lines |\n|---|---|---|\n| `checkout.session.completed` | `handleCheckoutSessionCompleted` | 56-57, 96-529 |\n| `customer.subscription.created` | `handleSubscriptionUpdate` | 61, 535-616 |\n| `customer.subscription.updated` | `handleSubscriptionUpdate` | 61, 535-616 |\n| `customer.subscription.deleted` | `handleSubscriptionDeleted` | 65-66, 621-643 |\n| `payment_intent.succeeded` | Log only | 69-71 |\n| `payment_intent.payment_failed` | Log only | 73-75 |\n| `invoice.payment_failed` | `handleInvoicePaymentFailed` | 77-79, 726-790 |\n| `charge.dispute.created` | `handleChargeDisputeCreated` | 81-83, 883-1002 |\n\n#### **C. Checkout Session Completed Handler** (lines 96-529)\n\n**1. Validation** (lines 101-104)\n   - Only process if `session.payment_status === 'paid'`\n   - Skip if not yet paid\n\n**2. Metadata Extraction** (lines 107-110)\n   - Extract: `locale`, `cart_items` (JSON string), `gift_message`\n\n**3. Session Details Retrieval** (lines 113-115)\n   - Expand: `['line_items', 'payment_intent', 'payment_intent.payment_method']`\n   - Get full session including line items and payment method type\n\n**4. Idempotency Check** (lines 151-162)\n   - **Query:** `SELECT * FROM orders WHERE stripe_session_id = ?`\n   - If order exists: Log \"skipping (idempotent)\" and return early\n   - **Key:** Prevents duplicate order creation on webhook retries\n\n**5. User Lookup** (lines 164-173)\n   - Query `users` table by `customer_email`\n   - Sets `userId` for later operations (or null for guest checkout)\n\n**6. Order Creation** (lines 175-200)\n\n   **Insert:**\n   ```sql\n   INSERT INTO orders (\n     user_id, stripe_session_id, stripe_payment_intent_id,\n     status='paid', total_cents, currency,\n     shipping_address (JSONB), customer_email, locale,\n     gift_message, payment_method, paid_at\n   )\n   ```\n\n   **Shipping Address JSONB:**\n   ```json\n   {\n     \"name\": \"string\",\n     \"line1\": \"street\",\n     \"line2\": \"apt (optional)\",\n     \"city\": \"string\",\n     \"state\": \"string\",\n     \"postal_code\": \"string\",\n     \"country\": \"string\"\n   }\n   ```\n\n**7. Order Items Creation** (lines 202-230)\n\n   - Map `lineItems` to `order_items`\n   - Extract from cart metadata: `product_id, variant_id, personalization_id, composition_id`\n   - Calculate `unit_price_cents = item.amount_total / item.quantity`\n   - **Constraint:** Filter out items without `product_id` OR `variant_id`\n   - Batch insert valid items\n\n**8. Notifications** (lines 232-270)\n\n   **Order Confirmation Notification:**\n   ```sql\n   INSERT INTO notifications (\n     user_id, type='order_confirmation',\n     title: \"Order #XXX Confirmed\",\n     body: \"X items • Total: €YY\",\n     data: { order_id, session_id, total_cents, currency, item_count },\n     is_read: false\n   )\n   ```\n   - Only for authenticated users (line 255)\n   - Guest checkouts skip notification (line 268-269)\n\n**9. Audit Log** (lines 272-298)\n\n   ```sql\n   INSERT INTO audit_log (\n     actor_type='webhook', actor_id='stripe_webhook',\n     action='order_created', resource_type='order',\n     changes: { status, total_cents, currency },\n     metadata: { stripe_session_id, stripe_payment_intent_id, customer_email, locale }\n   )\n   ```\n\n**10. Coupon Usage Counter Increment** (lines 300-321)\n\n   - Query `coupons` by `code` → get `times_used`\n   - Increment: `times_used + 1`\n   - Only if `coupon_code` in session metadata\n\n**11. POD Provider Order Submission** (lines 323-497)\n\n   **Guard Checks:**\n   - At least 1 valid order item (line 324)\n   - Shipping address present (line 324)\n   - Customer email present (line 324)\n\n   **Product/Variant Mapping:**\n   - Query `products`: fetch `provider_product_id` (line 334-337)\n   - Query `product_variants`: fetch `external_variant_id` (line 339-342)\n   - Check for missing mappings → if any item has no `external_variant_id`, mark order as `requires_review` (lines 349-366)\n\n   **Production URLs Resolution:**\n   - Build map from `cartItems[i].composition_id → production_urls` (lines 369-374)\n\n   **Provider Line Items:**\n   - Map to provider format: `{ product_id: external_id, variant_id: external_id, quantity, files? }`\n   - Include `files` array if custom design (composition_id) exists (lines 389-398)\n\n   **Create Provider Order:**\n   ```typescript\n   provider.createOrder({\n     internalOrderId: order.id,\n     label: `Order XXX`,\n     lineItems: [...],\n     shippingAddress: (canonicalized),\n     suppressShippingNotification: false\n   })\n   ```\n   - Returns `{ externalId }` (line 409-420)\n\n   **Update Order with Provider ID:**\n   ```sql\n   UPDATE orders SET\n     external_order_id=externalId,\n     pod_provider='printful',\n     status='submitted',\n     pod_last_attempt_at=NOW()\n   ```\n\n   **Submit for Production:**\n   - Call `provider.submitForProduction(externalId)` (line 443)\n   - **On error:** Set `status='requires_review'`, increment `pod_retry_count`, notify admin & customer (lines 445-467)\n\n   **Provider Error Handling:**\n   - Catch all provider errors (line 469)\n   - Mark order as `requires_review` or `requires_retry`\n   - Store `pod_error` message (line 480)\n   - **Notify:** Call `notifyAdminOfProviderFailure()` + send order issue email (lines 487-490)\n   - **Don't throw:** Webhook continues even if provider fails (line 492)\n\n**12. Order Confirmation Email** (lines 504-522)\n\n   - Call `sendOrderConfirmationEmail()` (line 508)\n   - **Parameters:**\n     ```typescript\n     {\n       to: customerEmail,\n       orderId: order.id,\n       orderNumber: order.id.slice(0, 8),\n       itemCount: validOrderItems.length,\n       totalCents: order.total_cents,\n       currency: order.currency,\n       locale: order.locale || 'en'\n     }\n     ```\n   - Non-blocking: errors logged, webhook continues\n\n**13. Credit Pack Purchases** (lines 499-502)\n\n   - Check `session.metadata.type === 'credit_pack'`\n   - Route to `handleCreditPackPurchase()` (line 501)\n\n#### **D. Subscription Update Handler** (lines 535-616)\n\n- Lookup user by `stripe_customer_id`\n- Update: `tier = 'premium'|'free'`, `subscription_status`, `subscription_period_end`\n- **On activation:** Add 10 bonus credits via RPC `add_credits(user_id, 10)` (line 580)\n- **Bonus tracking:** Insert `credit_transactions` record (lines 587-592)\n- **Drip sequence:** Trigger `triggerDripSequence('welcome')` via email service (line 606)\n\n#### **E. Credit Pack Purchase Handler** (lines 650-720)\n\n**Idempotency:**\n- Insert `credit_transactions` first with UNIQUE constraint `(user_id, stripe_payment_id)`\n- If UNIQUE violation (code 23505): Already processed, return early (line 670-672)\n\n**Atomic Balance Update:**\n- Call RPC `add_credits(user_id, amount)` → atomically increments `credit_balance`\n- Returns new balance (line 678-681)\n\n**Backfill Transaction:**\n- Update transaction record with `balance_after = newBalance` (line 686-690)\n\n**Email Notification:**\n- Call `sendCreditPurchaseEmail()` (line 704)\n\n#### **G. Invoice Payment Failed Handler** (lines 726-790)\n\n- Lookup user by `stripe_customer_id`\n- Update: `subscription_status='past_due'`\n- Send payment failure email via Resend (lines 753-773)\n- Send admin alert API call (lines 776-784)\n\n#### **H. Admin Notifications:**\n   - Create `notifications` for all admin users (type='chargeback')\n   - Include dispute reason, amount, currency (lines 955-978)\n\n**5. Order Confirmation Email** (lines 50-174)\n\n- **Subject:** Locale-aware (EN: \"Order Confirmation #XXX\", ES: \"Confirmación de Pedido #XXX\")\n- **Contains:** Order number, item count, total amount, currency\n- **Footer:** \"We will send shipping confirmation when order ships\"\n\n#### **D. Async Execution**\n\n- All emails sent without awaiting completion (webhook continues)\n- Errors logged but don't interrupt webhook flow\n\n---\n\n### 7. EDGE CASES & ERROR HANDLING\n\n#### **A. Out of Stock During Checkout**\n\n**Detection:** Stock validation before session creation (lines 91-133 in create-session route)\n- **Response:** 409 with `unavailableItems` array\n- **Frontend:** Shows toast with product names (CheckoutView line 264-268)\n- **User Action:** Must return to cart to remove items\n\n**Detection (Alternative):** At webhook time (if item goes OOS between checkout and webhook)\n- **Handling:** Provider order submission fails → order marked `requires_review`\n- **Notification:** Admin + customer notified via email\n\n#### **B. Cart Expiration**\n\n- No explicit cart expiration in checkout flow\n- Relies on variant availability check at payment time\n\n#### **C. Payment Failure**\n\n**Stripe-side failures:**\n- `payment_intent.payment_failed` event logged but not acted upon\n\n**Provider-side failures:**\n- `handleChargeDisputeCreated()` marks order as `cancelled` (line 915)\n- Fulfillment paused\n\n#### **D. Duplicate Order Prevention**\n\n**Idempotency check:** Query `orders` table by `stripe_session_id` (lines 151-162)\n- If exists, skip processing\n- **Key:** Prevents duplicate order creation if webhook retried\n\n#### **E. Guest vs Authenticated Checkout**\n\n| Aspect | Authenticated | Guest |\n|---|---|---|\n| Address | Saved addresses or new | Email only (Stripe collects address) |\n| User ID | Looked up from email | NULL |\n| Notification | Created in DB | Skipped (no user context) |\n| Order History | Visible in /orders | Not linked to account |\n\n#### **F. Coupon Edge Cases**\n\n- **Invalid coupon:** Silently ignored (not fatal to order)\n- **Expired coupon:** Not validated in create-session (server-side only checks in DB)\n- **Insufficient balance:** Cart total validated against `min_purchase_amount`\n- **Usage limit exceeded:** Checked before session creation\n\n#### **G. Missing Provider Variant Mapping**\n\n- **Detection:** Webhook checks if `external_variant_id` is null or '0' (line 350-351)\n- **Action:** Order marked `requires_review`, status NOT updated to `submitted`\n- **Notification:** Admin + customer notified (lines 361-364)\n- **Recovery:** Manual admin intervention required\n\n#### **H. Composition/Design File Resolution Failures**\n\n- **Scope:** Non-blocking, errors logged (lines 184-186)\n- **Impact:** Order created without custom files, provider gets blank areas\n- **Mitigation:** Webhook continues; order submitted without designs\n\n---\n\n### 8. SECURITY CONSIDERATIONS\n\n1. **Server-Side Price Authority:** Client prices overridden with DB prices (lines 189-213)\n2. **Webhook Signature Verification:** Stripe signature verified before any processing (line 41)\n3. **Rate Limiting:** Checkout session creation limited to 5/min per IP (line 33)\n4. **Idempotency:** Session-ID based deduplication prevents duplicate orders\n5. **RLS:** Supabase RLS active for user-owned data (addresses, orders)\n6. **Admin Client:** Service key bypasses RLS for order creation in webhook context\n\n---\n\n### 9.
